    ^ commit 165ae72400b2ceade95a20b94e40971dc6877890
    # This reduces the time spent premultiplying alpha by 61–79% by skipping the multiplication for fully opaque and fully transparent pixels. Fully opaque pixels remain unchanged, while fully transparent pixels are cleared.
    # 
    # Results of the new `premultiply_inplace` benchmark and the `load_from_memory` benchmark (the last row), on an Intel Core i7-10750H, Linux, default x86_64 target:
    # 
    # | Case | `bench` profile (`opt-level = 3`) | `production` profile (`opt-level = "s"`, fat LTO) |
    # |---|---|---|
    # | Opaque pixels | 3.07 -> 0.77 ms (-75%) | 3.00 -> 1.17 ms (-61%) |
    # | Transparent pixels | 3.07 -> 0.63 ms (-79%) | 3.02 -> 0.79 ms (-74%) |
    # | Translucent pixels | 2.93 -> 2.72 ms (-7%) | 2.84 -> 2.83 ms (within noise) |
    # | Decoding `palette.png` | 21.0 -> 13.7 ms (-35%) | 22.2 -> 16.7 ms (-25%) |
    # 
    # To reproduce: `./mach exec cargo bench -p servo-pixels --bench premultiply_inplace --bench load_from_memory`, with `--profile production` for the second column.
    # 
    # Testing: No behavior change. A new unit test covers every color under every alpha.

+https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/50059	(@mrobinson, #50059)	fonts: Make a `skrifa::Font` for every Servo `Font` (#50059)

    ^ commit 37ad71ff06d13bf89c82d5d4508ebd3542ecdf33
    ^ /!\ contains changes to WPT expectations! it probably affects the web platform
    # This change makes it so that every single `Font` in Servo carries a
    # `skrifa::Font`. This is an important step toward enabling HarfRust and
    # fixing bug #197. In addition, this will allow implementing font metrics
    # calculation and vertical metrics calculation in a platform-independent
    # way.
    # 
    # This allows a lot of really nice changes:
    # 
    # 1. The code to get a `FontTemplateDescriptor` for a web font is
    #    completely platform-independent now and based on an early created and
    #    cached `SkrifaFont`.
    # 2. Extraction of variation axes and font family name moves completely to
    #    `skrifa` and is now totally platform independent
    # 3. The decision about whether to apply synthetic bold to a font is now
    #    platform independent, which means that the same decision for
    #    synthetic oblique also only needs to be implemented a single time in
    #    the future. The rules for this is now consistent across platforms.
    # 
    # Testing: This gets one more WPT test passing due to increased
    # consistency in weight mapping.
    #

-https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/50100	(@dependabot[bot], @dependabot[bot], #50100)	build: bump org.jetbrains.kotlinx:kotlinx-serialization-json from 1.12.0 to 1.13.0 in /support/android/apk (#50100)
    ^ commit 946f3f1fa51b1389cf0f30a099e9251c6bef0007
    # Bumps [org.jetbrains.kotlinx:kotlinx-serialization-json](https://github.com/Kotlin/kotlinx.serialization) from 1.12.0 to 1.13.0.
    # <details>
    # <summary>Release notes</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/Kotlin/kotlinx.serialization/releases">org.jetbrains.kotlinx:kotlinx-serialization-json's releases</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>v1.13.0</h2>
    # <p>This release is based on Kotlin 2.4.10 and reverts a breaking change introduced in <a href="https://github.com/Kotlin/kotlinx.serialization/releases/tag/v1.12.0">1.12.0</a>.</p>
    # <p>Besides that, it improves <code>JsonElement.toString</code> performance.</p>
    # <h2>Exceptions rework rollback</h2>
    # <p>In 1.12.0, Json format started intercepting almost all exceptions thrown by serializers during serialization and deserialization, and wrapping them into a JsonException with additional information about JSON path and input.</p>
    # <p>The impact of the change was underestimated, and it was decided to revert the change.</p>
    # <h2>Performance improvements</h2>
    # <p><code>JsonElement.toString</code> was rewritten and its improved twofold on some benchmarks.  See <a href="https://redirect.github.com/Kotlin/kotlinx.serialization/issues/3296">#3296</a> and <a href="https://redirect.github.com/Kotlin/kotlinx.serialization/pull/3299">#3299</a> for details.</p>
    # </blockquote>
    # </details>
    # <details>
    # <summary>Changelog</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/Kotlin/kotlinx.serialization/blob/master/CHANGELOG.md">org.jetbrains.kotlinx:kotlinx-serialization-json's changelog</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h1>1.13.0 / 2026-11-10</h1>
    # <p>This release is based on Kotlin 2.4.10 and reverts a breaking change introduced in 1.12.0.</p>
    # <p>Besides that, it improves <code>JsonElement.toString</code> performance.</p>
    # <h2>Exceptions rework rollback</h2>
    # <p>In 1.12.0, Json format start intercepting almost all exceptions thrown by serializers during serialization and
    # deserialization, and wrap them into a <code>JsonException</code> with additional information about JSON path and input.</p>
    # <p>The impact of the change was underestimated, and it was decided to revert the change.</p>
    # <h2>Performance improvements</h2>
    # <p><code>JsonElement.toString</code> was rewritten and its improved twofold on some benchmarks.
    # See <a href="https://redirect.github.com/Kotlin/kotlinx.serialization/issues/3296">#3296</a> and <a href="https://redirect.github.com/Kotlin/kotlinx.serialization/pull/3299">#3299</a> for details.</p>
    # </blockquote>
    # </details>

+https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/38707	(@nicoburns, @mrobinson, #38707)	fonts: Add `harfrust` shaping backend (#38707)
    wip; HarfRust, a pure-Rust text shaping backend, can now be used instead of HarfBuzz
    ^ commit 2a3eb5b6af5c854042656c12a4d3c2bfe31fc206
    ^ /!\ contains libservo or embedder_traits changes! does it affect our public API?
    ^ /!\ contains servoshell changes! does it affect the user experience?
    ^ /!\ may contain changes to feature flags
    # This changes adds:
    # 
    # - An additional shaping backend using [HarfRust](https://github.com/harfbuzz/harfrust) in addition to the existing HarfBuzz based one.
    # - A "both" shaping backend that runs both the HarfBuzz and HarfRust shapers and diffs the output.
    # 
    # HarfRust is an under-development pure-rust port of HarfBuzz backed by Google. This is greatly helped by the APIs being very similar.
    # 
    # - Depends on: #38704
    # - Depends on: #38755
    # - Part of #33482
    # 
    # ## Performance
    # 
    # As of 2025-08-15, HarfRust was benchmarked as being ~20% slower than HarfBuzz (https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1lyPPZHXIF8gE0Tpx7_IscwhwaZa4KOpdt7vnV0jQT9o/preview). Work to reduce that gap is ongoing.
    # 
    # ## Feature flag incantations
    # 
    # - Build with HarfRust (default) `./mach build`
    # - Build with just HarfBuzz `./mach build -r --no-default-features --features bundled,harfbuzz,js_jit,gamepad`
    # - Build with both backends: `./mach build -r --features harfbuzz`
    # - With both backends compiled in:
    #     - Run with HarfRust (default) `./mach run`
    #     - Run with HarfBuzz `./mach run -- --pref fonts_shaping_backend=harfbuzz`
    #     - Run with debug comparison backend: `./mach run -- --pref fonts_shaping_backend=compare`

+https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49180	(@webbeef, @mrobinson, #49180)	script: Don't refocus the element that already has focus (#49180)
    perf; faster on Speedometer 3, especially TodoMVC-jQuery, by avoiding unnecessary refocusing and rooting
    ^ commit 66cc3968307264c408ff7e0db0dd869224da61f1
    # Per spec, the focusing steps return when the new focus target is already the
    # currently focused area, which Servo didn't implement. It gets the
    # focusable area of the target first, which needs the style of the
    # document and so may update its style and layout, and then gave the
    # target focus again and scrolled it into view, which may need layout too.
    # 
    # Without a fallback target, the focusing steps don't change the currently
    # focused area, whether it's still a focusable area or not, so check for
    # it first and return. Like Gecko, don't scroll it into view either.
    # 
    # Testing: This shouldn't change observable behavior, so is covered by
    # existing tests. This speeds up Speedometer significantly, especially the
    # TodoMVC-JQuery suite.
    # Fixes:  #50033.

    # Bumps the rustls-webpki-related group with 1 update: [rustls-webpki](https://github.com/rustls/webpki).
    # 
    # Updates `rustls-webpki` from 0.103.16 to 0.103.17
    # <details>
    # <summary>Release notes</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/rustls/webpki/releases">rustls-webpki's releases</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>0.103.17</h2>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Add <code>VerifiedPath::issuer_spki()</code> for accessing the encoded SPKI of a verified end entity certificate's issuer.</li>
    # <li>Add <code>spki_for_anchor()</code> for accessing the encoded SPKI of a parsed trust anchor.</li>
    # <li>Add <code>EndEntityCert::sct_log_timestamps()</code> for accessing an iterator of <code>sct::LogIdAndTimestamp</code> entries present in the certificate.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h2>What's Changed</h2>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Prepare 0.103.17, backport SCT work for upki by <a href="https://github.com/cpu"><code>@​cpu</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/rustls/webpki/pull/553">rustls/webpki#553</a></li>
    # </ul>
    # <p><strong>Full Changelog</strong>: <a href="https://github.com/rustls/webpki/compare/v/0.103.16...v/0.103.17">https://github.com/rustls/webpki/compare/v/0.103.16...v/0.103.17</a></p>
    # </blockquote>
    # </details>
    # <details>
    # <summary>Commits</summary>
    # <ul>
    # <li><a href="https://github.com/rustls/webpki/commit/7d4f8d83cc13d3f4169ef774aebb6319a376e000"><code>7d4f8d8</code></a> Prepare 0.103.17</li>
    # <li><a href="https://github.com/rustls/webpki/commit/0f08046f84b97dedeb9d2d20fa640640c3f54ec5"><code>0f08046</code></a> sct: continue reading SCTs after unsupported versions</li>
    # <li><a href="https://github.com/rustls/webpki/commit/713f038854dd5373851b933cbc69420bbb657149"><code>713f038</code></a> sct: helpers for test SCT encoding</li>

    # Updates `wgpu-core` from 30.0.1 to 30.0.2
    # <details>
    # <summary>Release notes</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/releases">wgpu-core's releases</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>v30.0.2</h2>
    # <h3>Bug Fixes</h3>
    # <h4>General</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Explicitly implement <code>Send</code> and <code>Sync</code> in <code>wgpu-core</code> to reduce the risk of <code>recursion_limit</code> overflow. By <a href="https://github.com/nazar-pc"><code>@​nazar-pc</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/9953">#9953</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix a texture id leak when surface acquire returns no texture. By <a href="https://github.com/jnb"><code>@​jnb</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10184">#10184</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix a use after free of a mapped buffer when its device is lost or destroyed. By <a href="https://github.com/uael"><code>@​uael</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10593">#10593</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>naga</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a compilation error when the <code>glsl-in</code> feature is enabled without <code>spv-in</code> or <code>wgsl-in</code>. By <a href="https://github.com/andyleiserson"><code>@​andyleiserson</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10297">#10297</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>Metal</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a crash/hang when configuring a surface with the <code>Bt2100Pq</code>, <code>Bt2100Hlg</code>, or <code>ExtendedDisplayP3</code> color space: the dynamically resolved CoreGraphics color-space constants were read with one level of indirection missing. By <a href="https://github.com/stuartparmenter"><code>@​stuartparmenter</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10175">#10175</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix oversaturated sRGB surface colors on wide-gamut displays by explicitly setting the layer's sRGB color space. By <a href="https://github.com/jinleili"><code>@​jinleili</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10286">#10286</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>WebGPU</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Report <code>CompositeAlphaMode::PreMultiplied</code> in the surface capabilities of the WebGPU backend. Surface configuration already accepted this mode. By <a href="https://github.com/elbaro"><code>@​elbaro</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10507">#10507</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # </blockquote>
    # </details>

    # <li>Explicitly implement <code>Send</code> and <code>Sync</code> in <code>wgpu-core</code> to reduce the risk of <code>recursion_limit</code> overflow.     # <li>Fix a texture id leak when surface acquire returns no texture. By <a href="https://github.com/jnb"><code>@​jnb</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10184">#10184</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix a use after free of a mapped buffer when its device is lost or destroyed. By <a href="https://github.com/uael"><code>@​uael</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10593">#10593</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>naga</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a compilation error when the <code>glsl-in</code> feature is enabled without <code>spv-in</code> or <code>wgsl-in</code>. By <a href="https://github.com/andyleiserson"><code>@​andyleiserson</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10297">#10297</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>Metal</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a crash/hang when configuring a surface with the <code>Bt2100Pq</code>, <code>Bt2100Hlg</code>, or <code>ExtendedDisplayP3</code> color space: the dynamically resolved CoreGraphics color-space constants were read with one level of indirection missing. By <a href="https://github.com/stuartparmenter"><code>@​stuartparmenter</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10175">#10175</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix oversaturated sRGB surface colors on wide-gamut displays by explicitly setting the layer's sRGB color space. By <a href="https://github.com/jinleili"><code>@​jinleili</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10286">#10286</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>WebGPU</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Report <code>CompositeAlphaMode::PreMultiplied</code> in the surface capabilities of the WebGPU backend. Surface configuration already accepted this mode. By <a href="https://github.com/elbaro"><code>@​elbaro</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10507">#10507</a>.</li>

    # Updates `naga-types` from 30.0.1 to 30.0.2
    # <details>
    # <summary>Release notes</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/releases">naga-types's releases</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>v30.0.2</h2>
    # <h3>Bug Fixes</h3>
    # <h4>General</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Explicitly implement <code>Send</code> and <code>Sync</code> in <code>wgpu-core</code> to reduce the risk of <code>recursion_limit</code> overflow. By <a href="https://github.com/nazar-pc"><code>@​nazar-pc</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/9953">#9953</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix a texture id leak when surface acquire returns no texture. By <a href="https://github.com/jnb"><code>@​jnb</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10184">#10184</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix a use after free of a mapped buffer when its device is lost or destroyed. By <a href="https://github.com/uael"><code>@​uael</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10593">#10593</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>naga</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a compilation error when the <code>glsl-in</code> feature is enabled without <code>spv-in</code> or <code>wgsl-in</code>. By <a href="https://github.com/andyleiserson"><code>@​andyleiserson</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10297">#10297</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>Metal</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a crash/hang when configuring a surface with the <code>Bt2100Pq</code>, <code>Bt2100Hlg</code>, or <code>ExtendedDisplayP3</code> color space: the dynamically resolved CoreGraphics color-space constants were read with one level of indirection missing. By <a href="https://github.com/stuartparmenter"><code>@​stuartparmenter</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10175">#10175</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix oversaturated sRGB surface colors on wide-gamut displays by explicitly setting the layer's sRGB color space. By <a href="https://github.com/jinleili"><code>@​jinleili</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10286">#10286</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>WebGPU</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Report <code>CompositeAlphaMode::PreMultiplied</code> in the surface capabilities of the WebGPU backend. Surface configuration already accepted this mode. By <a href="https://github.com/elbaro"><code>@​elbaro</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10507">#10507</a>.</li>

    # 
    # Updates `wgpu-core-deps-apple` from 30.0.1 to 30.0.2
    # <details>
    # <summary>Release notes</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/releases">wgpu-core-deps-apple's releases</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>v30.0.2</h2>
    # <h3>Bug Fixes</h3>
    # <h4>General</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Explicitly implement <code>Send</code> and <code>Sync</code> in <code>wgpu-core</code> to reduce the risk of <code>recursion_limit</code> overflow. By <a href="https://github.com/nazar-pc"><code>@​nazar-pc</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/9953">#9953</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix a texture id leak when surface acquire returns no texture. By <a href="https://github.com/jnb"><code>@​jnb</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10184">#10184</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix a use after free of a mapped buffer when its device is lost or destroyed. By <a href="https://github.com/uael"><code>@​uael</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10593">#10593</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>naga</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a compilation error when the <code>glsl-in</code> feature is enabled without <code>spv-in</code> or <code>wgsl-in</code>. By <a href="https://github.com/andyleiserson"><code>@​andyleiserson</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10297">#10297</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>Metal</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a crash/hang when configuring a surface with the <code>Bt2100Pq</code>, <code>Bt2100Hlg</code>, or <code>ExtendedDisplayP3</code> color space: the dynamically resolved CoreGraphics color-space constants were read with one level of indirection missing. By <a href="https://github.com/stuartparmenter"><code>@​stuartparmenter</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10175">#10175</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix oversaturated sRGB surface colors on wide-gamut displays by explicitly setting the layer's sRGB color space. By <a href="https://github.com/jinleili"><code>@​jinleili</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10286">#10286</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>WebGPU</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Report <code>CompositeAlphaMode::PreMultiplied</code> in the surface capabilities of the WebGPU backend. Surface configuration already accepted this mode. By <a href="https://github.com/elbaro"><code>@​elbaro</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10507">#10507</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # </blockquote>
    # </details>
    # <details>

    # <li>Fix a texture ID leak when surface acquire returns no texture. By <a href="https://github.com/jnb"><code>@​jnb</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10184">#10184</a>.</li>

    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a crash/hang when configuring a surface with the <code>Bt2100Pq</code>, <code>Bt2100Hlg</code>, or <code>ExtendedDisplayP3</code> color space: the dynamically resolved CoreGraphics color-space constants were read with one level of indirection missing.     # </ul>
    # <h4>WebGPU</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Report <code>CompositeAlphaMode::PreMultiplied</code> in the surface capabilities of the WebGPU backend. Surface configuration already accepted this mode. By <a href="https://github.com/elbaro"><code>@​elbaro</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10507">#10507</a>.</li>

    # Updates `wgpu-core-deps-emscripten` from 30.0.1 to 30.0.2
    # <details>
    # <summary>Release notes</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/releases">wgpu-core-deps-emscripten's releases</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>v30.0.2</h2>
    # <h3>Bug Fixes</h3>
    # <h4>General</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Explicitly implement <code>Send</code> and <code>Sync</code> in <code>wgpu-core</code> to reduce the risk of <code>recursion_limit</code> overflow. By <a href="https://github.com/nazar-pc"><code>@​nazar-pc</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/9953">#9953</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix a texture id leak when surface acquire returns no texture. By <a href="https://github.com/jnb"><code>@​jnb</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10184">#10184</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix a use after free of a mapped buffer when its device is lost or destroyed. By <a href="https://github.com/uael"><code>@​uael</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10593">#10593</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>naga</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a compilation error when the <code>glsl-in</code> feature is enabled without <code>spv-in</code> or <code>wgsl-in</code>. By <a href="https://github.com/andyleiserson"><code>@​andyleiserson</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10297">#10297</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>Metal</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a crash/hang when configuring a surface with the <code>Bt2100Pq</code>, <code>Bt2100Hlg</code>, or <code>ExtendedDisplayP3</code> color space: the dynamically resolved CoreGraphics color-space constants were read with one level of indirection missing. By <a href="https://github.com/stuartparmenter"><code>@​stuartparmenter</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10175">#10175</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix oversaturated sRGB surface colors on wide-gamut displays by explicitly setting the layer's sRGB color space. By <a href="https://github.com/jinleili"><code>@​jinleili</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10286">#10286</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>WebGPU</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Report <code>CompositeAlphaMode::PreMultiplied</code> in the surface capabilities of the WebGPU backend. Surface configuration already accepted this mode. By <a href="https://github.com/elbaro"><code>@​elbaro</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10507">#10507</a>.</li>

    # Updates `wgpu-hal` from 30.0.1 to 30.0.2
    # <details>
    # <summary>Release notes</summary>
    # <p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/releases">wgpu-hal's releases</a>.</em></p>
    # <blockquote>
    # <h2>v30.0.2</h2>
    # <h3>Bug Fixes</h3>
    # <h4>General</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Explicitly implement <code>Send</code> and <code>Sync</code> in <code>wgpu-core</code> to reduce the risk of <code>recursion_limit</code> overflow. By <a href="https://github.com/nazar-pc"><code>@​nazar-pc</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/9953">#9953</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix a texture id leak when surface acquire returns no texture. By <a href="https://github.com/jnb"><code>@​jnb</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10184">#10184</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix a use after free of a mapped buffer when its device is lost or destroyed. By <a href="https://github.com/uael"><code>@​uael</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10593">#10593</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>naga</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a compilation error when the <code>glsl-in</code> feature is enabled without <code>spv-in</code> or <code>wgsl-in</code>. By <a href="https://github.com/andyleiserson"><code>@​andyleiserson</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10297">#10297</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>Metal</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Fix a crash/hang when configuring a surface with the <code>Bt2100Pq</code>, <code>Bt2100Hlg</code>, or <code>ExtendedDisplayP3</code> color space: the dynamically resolved CoreGraphics color-space constants were read with one level of indirection missing. By <a href="https://github.com/stuartparmenter"><code>@​stuartparmenter</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10175">#10175</a>.</li>
    # <li>Fix oversaturated sRGB surface colors on wide-gamut displays by explicitly setting the layer's sRGB color space. By <a href="https://github.com/jinleili"><code>@​jinleili</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10286">#10286</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # <h4>WebGPU</h4>
    # <ul>
    # <li>Report <code>CompositeAlphaMode::PreMultiplied</code> in the surface capabilities of the WebGPU backend. Surface configuration already accepted this mode. By <a href="https://github.com/elbaro"><code>@​elbaro</code></a> in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/gfx-rs/wgpu/pull/10507">#10507</a>.</li>
    # </ul>
    # </blockquote>
    # </details>

+https://github.com/servo/servo/pull/49693	(@ZettaScript, #49693)	Make aws-lc-rs optional (#49693)
    api; embedders can now disable the aws-lc-rs feature and choose their own TLS crypto provider
    ^ commit f703f9374ed109083d2a3b463216d46c78679aa0
    ^ /!\ contains libservo or embedder_traits changes! does it affect our public API?
    # This introduces the feature `aws-lc-rs` (enabled by default). When disabled, the embedder has to setup RusTLS's crypto provider themselves. Then, `aws-lc-rs` is absent from the dependency graph. For this, I had to switch from `aws-lc-rs` to RustCrypto for HMAC and SHA2 (re-using crates that were already used by lots of dependencies anyway).
    # 
    # Testing: Existing tests cover the default aws-lc-rs case. Should there be tests without the feature (e.g. with ring)?
    # Fixes: #37910 
    #

    ^ commit d22050dbbf0d5d7544df2f2a77bea18c4038a027
    ^ /!\ contains changes to WPT expectations! it probably affects the web platform

    # 
    # Testing: It builds.
